# Agent 2 — Tenders & Procurement — Findings 2026-08-01

(Method: shared research harness — one agent per procurement portal, adversarial per-claim verification, completeness gate, DETERMINISTIC table render. 15 procurement portal(s) targeted, 15 returned signals, 267 verified signals. Confidence capped at Medium per single-agent playbook. Table rows ordered by Printec value (size x win), biddable first.)
(Run note: the first harness pass lost 27 of 54 agents to API disconnects and a session limit; 7 procurement portals died at the verify stage AFTER their research checkpoints had been written to disk. A second, checkpoint-seeded recovery pass re-verified and extended those 7, and Slovenia was verified in 3 batches because its 29 signals exceeded the 32k output-token cap. The passes are merged by script (weekly-intelligence/scripts/merge_harness_runs.py) — the table is machine-rendered, never LLM-written — and 24 duplicate rows (the same notice found by both TED and a national portal) were merged on TED/Prozorro notice id)

## What changed since last run

The single most important resolution this run is the Ukrgasbank ATM award that the previous cycle flagged as lost against Printec: it is confirmed from the primary Prozorro record as going to **TOV "ATM sistems" (EDRPOU 44372551) at UAH 49,720,800 incl. VAT on 16/04/2026** for 81 machines (36 through-the-wall, 37 lobby, 8 full-cycle recyclers) — a small Ukrainian local player, exactly the tier of competitor the playbook says not to discount, and the name Agent 6 needs. Second, PrivatBank's recycling-ATM programme was located where Prozorro cannot see it: tender **PB-2026-03-17-3068329 "Ресайклінговий банкомат (1 етап)"** is live on the bank's own PrivatTender portal and has sat in pre-qualification since 08/04/2026, after a first attempt was cancelled to rewrite the technical requirements — against a stated plan of 800 ATMs in 2026 and a re-verified Printec incumbency (UAH 838,639,173.73 award of 17/01/2025, plus 39,000 Castles POS terminals in October 2025). Third, Printec turns up as a *winner* in a market we were not tracking for it: **PRINTEC S.I. d.o.o. holds the Pošta Slovenije parcel-locker supply and five-year maintenance contract**. Fourth, the most directly biddable new cash-automation tender is **Banka Slovenije's banknote-sorter maintenance, open to 17/08/2026**, where verification newly identified the incumbent as Giesecke+Devrient on a predecessor contract awarded at EUR 963,000 against a EUR 1.23m estimate — on a single bid, which is both the benchmark and the opening.

Structurally, three demand drivers hardened. Slovakia's cashless-acceptance mandate is visibly moving hardware — POS terminals grew from 70,943 to 77,711 between 31/12/2025 and 31/05/2026 (+9.5%) while the ATM fleet shrank but shifted toward deposit-capable machines. Hungary's ATM mandate survived the change of government, leaving roughly 600 ATMs to install in 500–1,000-population settlements by 31/12/2026, allocated bank-by-bank by MNB decree rather than by tender. And Croatia's shared national ATM network was created in legislation, so it will never appear in a procurement feed at all. Alongside these, Geldservice Austria's EUR 9.1m combined deposit-and-withdrawal recycler framework — expressly unlimited in term, with delivery into Slovenia and neighbouring markets — is the largest single cash-automation contest surfaced this run and is already in evaluation.

Verification corrected nine claims and left five unconfirmed. Notably, PrivatBank's payShield HSM count was corrected from six to seven procurements across two intermediaries; a claimed Slovenian award notice (AXESS/DESIGNA) was found not to exist on TED and was struck; Croatia's HAC card-acceptance tender is unresolved five months past its deadline with its estimate roughly tripled on identical scope; and Hrvatska pošta's POS-maintenance reference 26.2.83, carried as an open item since June, could not be found anywhere in TED and is now flagged rather than repeated. On the baseline: the Greek G-Cloud tender remains open and closes 03/08/2026, the Ministry of Culture IT tender 10/08/2026, and the thermal-imaging camera tender's deadline is confirmed moved to 26/08/2026. ELTA's counter estate hardened *against* us — its open procedure 3726 for operational leasing of 300 banknote counters was awarded to incumbent BOOS S.A.

## Top opportunities (ranked)

1. **PrivatBank (Ukraine)** — deposit/recycling ATMs for the 800-unit 2026 refresh; bid via PrivatTender, not Prozorro, and defend the incumbency now while stage 1 sits in pre-qualification (XL, High).
2. **ELTA / Hellenic Post (Greece)** — self-service and counter automation behind the 2026-2030 Development Plan and the all-bank fee-free ATM concept across ~450 banking-enabled locations; Cashflex/KEA is the natural vehicle (XL, Medium).
3. **Banca Transilvania (Romania)** — ATM/recycler refresh and managed services against a primary-sourced estate of 2,025 ATMs, 696 of them multifunctional (XL, Medium).
4. **Croatia shared national ATM network (HUB / Ministry of Finance / HNB)** — pooled-ATM operating model and managed services; pre-tender positioning, since the law creates it outside any portal (XL, Medium).
5. **Geldservice Austria** — combined deposit/withdrawal recyclers plus maintenance, EUR 9.1m over five years on an unlimited-term framework, delivery into Slovenia as well as Austria (L, Low).
6. **Banka Slovenije (Slovenia)** — banknote-sorter preventive/corrective maintenance, closing 17/08/2026; displace a single-bid Giesecke+Devrient incumbency benchmarked at EUR 963,000 (L, Medium).
7. **Bulgarian Posts (Bulgaria)** — banknote and coin counting machines in five lots to run lev-to-euro exchange through the post-office network; direct cash-automation product fit (L, Low).
8. **Sense Bank (Ukraine)** — ATM cassette repair, two lots (Wincor / NCR), UAH 432,200, closing 10/08/2026; small but immediately biddable field-service scope (S, High).

| Croatia — national shared ATM network (HUB / Ministry of Finance / HNB) | Croatia | THE BIGGEST CROATIAN ATM SIGNAL OF THIS RUN, and one no procurement portal shows — but the legal framing needs correcting. Croatia enacted 'Zakon o izmjenama i dopunama Zakona o usporedivosti naknada, prebacivanju računa za plaćanje i pristupu osnovnom računu' (Narodne novine 105/2025, doc. 1476, adopted 15/07/2025, published 23/07/2025), in force 01/01/2026. Consumers on the free-services package get fee-free cash withdrawal at their own bank's counter or ATM (channel chosen by the bank). Separately, new Article 27.e enters force on 01/01/2027 and obliges every credit institution to allow two fee-free withdrawals per month at ANY OTHER credit institution's ATM in Croatia. That obligation is UNCONDITIONAL in the statute — the law contains no reference to a 'nacionalna bankomatska mreža' and does not make Article 27.e contingent on one. The shared-network project is a separate, non-statutory track: the Ministry of Finance proposed a memorandum of understanding between the banks, the Ministry and HNB; Minister Marko Primorac said on 22/05/2025 that no agreement was reached on the memorandum text, so the law was pushed through instead, with banks having until 01/01/2027 to establish a network voluntarily. HNB's preferred model reportedly had FINA taking over ATM management. As of 27/04/2026 press reporting says (unofficially) that an Ernst & Young study produced THREE operating models and a final decision was expected 'within several weeks'; HUB called it an extremely complex, multi-layered project requiring technical, operational and legal alignment of many stakeholders. Market sizing in the same reporting, attributed to HNB: 106 million ATM transactions worth EUR 19.24 billion, 3,816 active ATMs (over 4,700 in summer), roughly 1,000 ATMs already in the MBNet shared network, and about EUR 380 million of bank revenue (~40% of retail commissions and fees) exposed. Uptake of the free package is low: 108,171 packages opened by late April 2026, 1.6% of 5.9 million consumer accounts. The operator of any shared network is UNDECIDED. (Zákon o evidencii tržieb) | Slovakia | Re-verified in the consolidated statute: § 15 'Bezhotovostná platba' obliges every seller subject to eKasa sales-recording to let the buyer pay CASHLESSLY on any transaction ABOVE EUR 1. Compliance is met by ANY 'platobný prostriedok' (§ 15(2)), explicitly including a QR-code payment order (§ 15(3)), for which the Financial Directorate supplies the technical means for the eKasa till to confirm the payment; if confirmation fails the till records that fact. Exemption where there is no internet signal or the confirmation service is down. Penalty for breaching § 15: nothing on first detection (instruction only), EUR 500–15,000 on the second detection (§ 17(1)(h)), EUR 3,000–40,000 on every further detection (§ 17(2)(a)). | MNB decree 19/2025 (VI. 26.) - bank-by-bank ATM allocation | Hungary | MNB decree 19/2025 (VI. 26.), issued under Act XVIII of 2025 (ATMtv.) and NGM decree 16/2025 (V. 29.), names settlement-by-settlement which payment service provider must install and operate an ATM. Annex 1 distributes 80 settlement packages across nine banks (CIB 1-5, ERSTE 6-13, K&H 14-22, MagNet 23, MBH 24-38, Raiffeisen 39-43, UniCredit 44-47, OTP 48-80, plus GRÁNIT). Press tallies of the resulting workload differ by measure: counted as SETTLEMENTS the split is OTP 364, MBH 217, K&H 117, Erste 103, Raiffeisen 62, CIB 53, UniCredit 51, Gránit 13, MagNet 13 = 993 settlements (Bank360, 27/06/2025); counted as ATM UNITS to install it is OTP 428, MBH 195, K&H 117, Erste 104, Raiffeisen 65, CIB 52, UniCredit 52, Gránit 13, MagNet 13 = 1,039 machines (Bankmonitor, 27/06/2025). Allocation is driven by a weighted cost indicator (debit cards issued, cash-related fee income, consumer payment accounts, total assets, retail deposits). Verified from the consolidated decree text: under §3(2) banks may swap settlement packages or assume each other's installation/operation obligations by contract; such contracts had to be concluded by 20/07/2025 for Phase-1 settlements and 28/02/2026 for Phase-2 (500-1,000 population) settlements, with notification to MNB via the ERA system within 5 working days and no later than 31/07/2025 and 16/03/2026 respectively. Any operation-assumption contract must run at least 5 years ('5 évnél rövidebb időtartamra nem köthető'). | Government decree 129/2025 (VI. 5.) - ATM relocation and decommissioning rules | Hungary | The absolute freeze on removing existing ATMs was relaxed. Government decree 129/2025 (VI. 5.) 'az automata bankjegykiadó gépek MŰKÖDÉSÉNEK veszélyhelyzeti fenntartásáról' was published in Magyar Közlöny 2025/67 and entered into force on 06/06/2025, simultaneously repealing 49/2025 (III. 24.). Verified rule: a bank operating an ATM may change the operating location of an existing machine - including when replacing the device - only if an ATM continues to operate in the same settlement (or district). Further reported grounds, from a press summary and not yet re-read in the decree text: where technical constraints prevent an in-place upgrade the replacement must go elsewhere in the same settlement/district; removal is permitted to redeploy to an underserved area, to rebalance within the same location on demand statistics, or on termination of the site lease. NEN N006/26/V00011619, CPV 30231100-8, published on profile 23/04/2026, deadline 15/05/2026 10:00. The spec drew heavy supplier pushback: five clarifications and two amendments to the tender documents between 28/04/2026 and 07/05/2026. A SELECTION NOTICE ('Uveřejnění oznámení o výběru') was published on 20/07/2026 — a supplier has been chosen, even though the NEN header status field still reads 'Not terminated' as of 01/08/2026. Grounds: the obligation is economically unjustified, disproportionately restricts freedom of enterprise and the right to property, and discriminatorily interferes with market processes. ## Verification notes
- **Corrected/contradicted by the adversarial pass (9):** MNB decree 19/2025 (VI. 26.) - bank-by-bank ATM allocation — The decree MECHANICS are confirmed against the consolidated text on net.jogtar.hu: swap/assumption right, deadlines 20/07/2025 and 28/02/2026, ERA notification within 5 working days but no later than 31/07/2025 and 16/03/2026, and the minimum 5-year term. The PER-BANK NUMBERS ARE WRONG. The researcher gave OTP 383, MBH 147, K&H 102, Erste 93, Raiffeisen 64, CIB 52, UniCredit 46, Gránit 13, MagNet 13 over '~913 settlements'. Neither published tally matches. Bank360 27/06/2025 (https://bank360.hu/blog/megszuletett-a-lista-melyik-telepulesre-melyik-bank-telepit-atm-et) gives OTP 364 / MBH 217 / K&H 117 / Erste 103 / Raiffeisen 62 / CIB 53 / UniCredit 51 / Gránit 13 / MagNet 13 over 993 settlements. Bankmonitor 27/06/2025 (https://bankmonitor.hu/mediatar/cikk/megvan-melyik-banknak-hol-kell-atm-et-telepitenie/) gives OTP 428 / MBH 195 / K&H 117 / Erste 104 / Raiffeisen 65 / CIB 52 / UniCredit 52 / Gránit 13 / MagNet 13 = 1,039 ATMs. Signal text rewritten to carry both published tallies and flag that they measure different things. Also corrected: the researcher cited a getpdf URL; I verified against the readable consolidated text at net.jogtar.hu/jogszabaly?docid=a2500019.mnb. Annex 1 is structured as 80 settlement PACKAGES, not 913 or 993 individual rows.; Banka Slovenije (central bank) - below-threshold (evidencna) purchases 2025 — CONTENT CONFIRMED, DATE CORRECTED. Re-downloaded the CSV on 02/08/2026: all three lines are verbatim - Utimaco HSM EUR 25,162 / CREAPLUS; VoP EUR 32,705 / BANK OF LATVIA; security operations centre EUR 20,332 / SINTAL. However the original source_date of 07/03/2026 is WRONG: the portal's period list (evidencnaNarocila/obdobjeGetList) shows 07/03/2026 is the statutory reporting DEADLINE for the 2025 period, while the Banka Slovenije return itself (porociloGetList?idObdobje=2025, id 50326, oddano=true) carries mdTs 25/02/2026. source_date corrected to 25/02/2026. Added corroboration re-verified this run: the Banka Slovenije PKI decision PDF (dossier 602928, published 20/07/2026) names CREAPLUS, resitve za varno poslovanje, d.o.o. as winner at EUR 372,827.12 incl. VAT - a materially larger CREAplus win than anything previously logged.; BIROFORM DNS d.o.o. / PIGO d.o.o. / Giesecke+Devrient - the Slovenian cash-device channel — I re-downloaded both evidenčna naročila CSVs and the three award notices on 02/08/2026. Records (a), (b) and (c) are confirmed on value and vendor. TWO CONTRADICTIONS. 1) The researcher quoted item (b) as 'Nakup in triletno vzdrževanje aparatov za preverjanje euro bankovcev'. CORRECTED VALUE - the register entry continues '...in aparatov za štetje in sortiranje euro kovancev', i.e. the EUR 24,160 also covers euro COIN counting and sorting machines (source: enarocanje.si evidenčna naročila CSV id 49886, retrieved 02/08/2026). 2) The researcher's core inference - 'no international cash-automation vendor present ... an unusually thin field for a Eurosystem country' - is FALSE. CORRECTED: Giesecke+Devrient GmbH, Prinzregentenstrasse 159, Munich holds Banka Slovenije's 'Nadgradnja obstoječe programske opreme na dveh sortirnih sistemih BPS M7', estimate EUR 356,557.37, awarded EUR 350,500 excl. VAT on a single bid, award notice JN006991/2024-EUe29/01 of 19/02/2025, TED 110277-2025, contract 2.09.3.1.02-16/2024-11 of 11/02/2025 (source: enarocanje.si, re-checked 02/08/2026). ADDED and independently verified: the FURS JN 25/2025 BAN banknote-counter/counterfeit-detection award to PIGO d.o.o. (EUR 7,560 excl. VAT, EUR 9,223.20 incl. VAT, contract C1620-25-000056 of 22/05/2025, buyer reg. 2482711000), which the researcher asserted in passing without citing it; and LPT's full legal name. Source_date is the retrieval date because the evidenčna registers are rolling files with no publication date of their own.; Banka Slovenije (central bank) — I re-downloaded Sklep.pdf (6 pages, 220 kB) via the portal file API and re-extracted it with pdfminer.six on 02/08/2026. CONTRADICTED: the researcher wrote 'corrected 11/05/2026, offers due 15/05/2026'. CORRECTED VALUE - 15/05/2026 was the ORIGINAL deadline; the 11/05/2026 corrective notice JN002488/2026-EUe16/01-P01 (TED 320269-2026) extended it to 29/05/2026 10:00, opening 29/05/2026 12:00 (source: enarocanje.si API obrazecGet ids 1043341 and 1082088, re-checked 02/08/2026). ADDED: ID SHOP's Lot 1 price excl. VAT is EUR 66,605.09 (the researcher gave only the VAT-inclusive 81,258.21); PALISADA named PAN Elektronik d.o.o. and ASSA ABLOY Opening Solutions Slovenija d.o.o. as subcontractors; K-HAUS was excluded for unpaid tax obligations under Art. 75(2) ZJN-3, NOLIMAL for omitting 14 required forms. Everything else - internal ref 2.09.3.1.01-8/2026, TED 231821-2026, 03/04/2026, Senat 2 of 10/07/2026, lot titles, the four bidder names, the 'will not repeat' statement - is confirmed verbatim. The predecessor procedure JN009796/2025 was independently confirmed (API obrazecGet?id=1016449): both lots one bid, neither awarded. NOTE: the PDF's price table is ambiguous on whether any priced bid existed for Lot 2, so I have not restated per-bidder Lot 2 values.; Česká pošta, s.p. / PHOBOS, spol. s r.o. — Downloaded and parsed TED 62496-2026 directly. The CZK 6,700,000 and the PHOBOS winner are correct, but the framing is wrong: the notice title is 'Čerpání z RD za období 1.10. - 31.12.2025' (drawdown from the framework agreement for the period 1 Oct - 31 Dec 2025), and the framework's own maximum value is CZK 250,000,000 excl. VAT with start 14/06/2018 and duration 96 months. So the original understated the estate by a factor of roughly 37 and mischaracterised a quarterly drawdown as an award. Also added the empty-weight award criterion, which is the single most useful detail in the notice for a displacement argument. Caveat: TED's framework-creating-notice pointer is inconsistent across this series (it cites 46552-2025 while the previous notice is 27703-2024 and a 2017 notice 385718-2017 carries the same CZK 250m ceiling), so the exact framework lineage and true end date should be confirmed from the successor notice before being quoted externally.; Government decree 129/2025 (VI. 5.) - ATM relocation and decommissioning rules — CORRECTED DATE: the researcher wrote 'in force from 07/06/2025'. Jogászvilág's Magyar Közlöny digest (https://jogaszvilag.hu/szakma/jogszabalyfigyelo-2025-23-het/) states the decree was published in Magyar Közlöny 2025/67 and entered into force on 06/06/2025, repealing 49/2025 (III. 24.) at the same moment. CORRECTED TITLE: it is 'az automata bankjegykiadó gépek MŰKÖDÉSÉNEK veszélyhelyzeti fenntartásáról' (operation), not 'üzemeltetésének' - confirmed verbatim in the MNB methodological guide's definitions section, which also independently confirms 49/2025 is repealed. SOURCE UPGRADED from the biztosdontes.hu blog post the researcher cited to the net.jogtar consolidated text plus a legal digest. CONFIRMED substantively: relocation is permitted only if an ATM keeps operating in the same settlement or district. NOT RE-VERIFIED: the three further removal grounds (technical constraints, redeployment to underserved area, rebalancing on demand statistics, lease termination) - net.jogtar.hu and njt.hu both refused connection on repeated attempts, the same failure the researcher logged. Signal text now separates verified from press-only.; Inspekce silniční dopravy (Czech Road Transport Inspectorate) — The full technical specification is CONFIRMED verbatim against the NEN page — every clause the original listed is present, including D+2, gross settlement, 100 destination accounts, 13-character display, 10-character alphanumeric identifier and meal-voucher blocking, plus extras the original omitted (PCI DSS, AES/SSL-TLS, geolocation/remote block, 7/24 helpdesk, training terminal). But the STATUS is wrong: the original said 'status still Not terminated... award or cancellation imminent'. The NEN publication log shows a selection notice was published on 20/07/2026, i.e. a supplier was already chosen twelve days before the research date. The header status field is stale, which is a recurring NEN quirk also seen on two other signals in this set. Corrected likelihood, and set win to '—' because this round is closed. Also corrected the CPV — NEN records 30231100-8 (computer terminals), not a banking-services code.; Bankart d.o.o. / IDEMIA Public Security France - Slovenian processing and biometrics incumbents — I re-downloaded the full 2024/2025/2026 contract-register CSVs (21.6 MB / 24.0 MB / 14.8 MB) on 02/08/2026 and filtered them myself. CONTRADICTED: the researcher wrote the BS-Bankart contract covers 'Sklop 1, Sklop 2, Sklop 3'. CORRECTED VALUE - the register's contract title is 'POGODBA O PROCESIRANJU IN PORAVNAVI SEPA PLAČIL - SKLOP 1, SKLOP 2, SKLOP 3, SKLOP 4, SKLOP 5, SKLOP 6', i.e. SIX lots (source: enarocanje.si contract register FY2024 CSV, contract 4600002301, retrieved 02/08/2026). All monetary figures, dates and counterparties otherwise matched exactly; note every register value is VAT-INCLUSIVE (column PogodbenaVrednostZDDVEUR), which the original signal did not state. ADDED: contract numbers and terms (both Bankart contracts end 31/01/2029), Bankart's address, procedure refs, and the IDEMIA legal-name change from 'Idemia Identity & Security France' to 'IDEMIA Public Security France' between the 2024 and 2025 annexes - use the current name for competitor records. FLAGGED AS UNVERIFIED: 'Bankart is Slovenia's single card/payment processor and ... decides what terminals certify' is an inference with no support in any primary source I checked; softened above. Source_date set to the most recent contract in the cited extract (30/04/2026); the register itself is a rolling file retrieved 02/08/2026.; Czech public-sector cash and payments procurement — STRUCTURAL FINDING (REVISED) — I re-ran the searches myself on the same portal. 'bankomat' returns 'No records to be displayed in this list' — CONFIRMED. 'POS termin' returns the same — CONFIRMED but misleading, because it is not the term Czech buyers use. 'počítačk' returns exactly 10 procedures — CONFIRMED, matching the original count precisely. However 'platebn' returns a full results page (paginated at 50) and 'samoobslu' returns 15, so the sweep was NOT exhaustive and the headline conclusion 'there is NO tender-driven route into the Czech ATM, recycler or POS market' is false for POS. Named the specific counter-examples above, the most significant being the Financial Administration's above-threshold terminal-and-gateway contract, now lapsed. Confidence dropped to Low because the finding's evidentiary method was shown to be keyword-fragile — the revised version should itself be re-tested before anyone acts on it. Keeping the row because the ATM half is sound and the correction is more valuable than a deletion. The sub-claim that Czech Post's NEN profile carries only two laptop tenders could not be reproduced and is unverified; what IS confirmed, from TED, is that Czech Post's buyer profile is tenderarena.cz/profil/detail.jsf?identifikator=Cp.
- **Unconfirmed (single/weak source, kept at Low) (5):** Banka Slovenije (central bank) - cash logistics / AKTIVA VAROVANJE d.d., Javni zavod Lekarna Ljubljana (Ljubljana municipal pharmacy chain), Financna uprava RS (FURS) / Posta Slovenije d.o.o., HP – Hrvatska pošta d.d. – ref 26.2.83 POS maintenance, MBH / Erste / K&H / OTP and the Act XVIII of 2025 ATM rollout - public-procurement coverage verdict


## Access issues & operator requests (per procurement portal)

| procurement portal | Access issue / what failed & the fallback used |
|---|---|
| TED (EU-wide) | None on TED. All 24 notice PDFs at https://ted.europa.eu/en/notice/<id>/pdf returned 200 to a plain GET with a desktop Chrome User-Agent and extracted cleanly with pdfminer.six. The TED Search API v3 (POST https://api.ted.europa.eu/v3/notices/search, public, no auth) accepted every query issued, including buyer-name and title full-text expressions. Note for future runs: the API returns notice titles in a rotating UI language (Hungarian in this run) regardless of the requested scope — the notice number and the PDF are the reliable artefacts, not the returned title string. gv.vergabeportal.at (the Austrian portal holding the GSA tender documents) was NOT opened this run; Claude-in-Chrome is not connected, so no browser escalation was attempted. |
| Greece — Diavgeia / KIMDIS / ESIDIS | Carried unchanged from the checkpoint. promitheus.gov.gr returns HTTP 302 to portal.eprocurement.gov.gr, which renders an empty body to a plain fetch (JavaScript application) — logged, not silently dropped; the working substitute route is contracts.gr per-CPV and per-organisation URLs. contracts.gr itself returns HTTP 404 on /v1/cpv/code/30123600-2 and /v1/cpv/code/30123600 and HTTP 500 on /v1/cpv/code/30123600-2/related, so coin/banknote-handling machinery could not be listed by CPV. api.diavgeia.gov.gr does not resolve from this host (DNS ENOTFOUND); et.diavgeia.gov.gr/luminapi/api/search/export returned HTTP 400 to the query form tried, so per-organisation Diavgeia API filtering remains unproven. Working Diavgeia routes stay https://diavgeia.gov.gr/opendata/search.json?subject=<term> for listing and https://diavgeia.gov.gr/doc/<ADA> for documents; /doc serves binary PDFs the fetch tool cannot read, but they save to disk and pdfminer.six extracts them cleanly (system pdftotext is not installed). One Diavgeia doc URL (ADA ΨΜ7ΒΟΡΡ2-ΝΕΑ) 302-redirected to http://static.old.diavgeia.gov.gr/doc/<ADA>. Bash has no network access on this host, so all fetching went through WebFetch. www.bankofgreece.gr/enimerosi/prokhryxeis remains HTTP 404; the live route is the announcements search. Claude-in-Chrome was not required. No new access attempts were made in this resumed run. |

GetCANoticeList (award notices) silently ignores its contractObject/contractTitle filter and GetCNoticeList silently ignores caNameFN, both returning the unfiltered 3,000-row cap - so authority-scoped and award-winner queries are not possible through this API and were worked around with keyword filtering. The notice-detail endpoints https://e-licitatie.ro/api-pub/PublicNotice/getCNoticeById/<id> and .../getCNoticeV2/<id> both return HTTP 404; the public notice pages are an Angular SPA and are not fetchable as text, so all notice data here comes from the list endpoints. https://www.cec.ro/achizitii returns 403 to the plain fetch tool but 200 with a desktop User-Agent (route used). https://www.bancatransilvania.ro/news/comunicate-de-presa/... returned HTTP 403 to WebFetch; worked around by using the BVB-filed interim report PDF instead and extracting it locally with pdfminer.six. |

TED API returns HTTP 429 under rapid querying — pacing of 8-20s between calls was required. Claude-in-Chrome not connected in the run that produced these findings. |

**Operator requests — gated / paywalled / blocked sources to fetch or verify manually:**
- **TED (EU-wide):** 1) Austrian procurement portal gv.vergabeportal.at/Detail/244277 — the Geldservice Austria EUR 9.1m "Kombinierte Ein- und Auszahlungsgeraete" (cash/coin recycler) tender documents and, critically, the list of candidates invited to the second stage. Registration is likely required, so an operator should retrieve the Ausschreibungsunterlage G26146096 and any Teilnehmerliste. This is the single highest-value item in this run's findings. 2) Slovak UVO/EVO portal — NBS 2026 procurement plan items 0003, 0131, 0132 and 0236, which are absent from TED and probably sub-threshold. 3) FINA Croatia (nabava@fina.hr, contact Marina Dolcic) — the re-issue timetable for the cancelled "Brojaci kovanica" coin-counter tender, ref 2.2.1/2026/V/I2.
- **Greece — Diavgeia / KIMDIS / ESIDIS:** 1) Whether ELTA has exercised the 200-machine option on tender 3726 (EUR 3,850/month excl. VAT) and on what trigger — decides whether the BOOS S.A. estate is 300 or 500 machines. 2) The ELTA-Eurobank Cooperation Agreement and its 30/04/2025 amendment, which the ELTA award decision of 21/05/2026 cites as the obligation behind the counter-equipment lease — it should specify what equipment, cash-handling and compliance duties ELTA carries, and is the key to sizing the ATM phase. 3) The ELTA 2026-2030 Development Plan with its capex annex. 4) KIMDIS notice 25PROC016452145 / original 25PROC016252913 (ODAP, EUR 1,295,800, ticket machines + POS software) and its award decision naming the winner. 5) ELTA Supply Contract F.P. 6276/2016 with BOOS S.A. 6) A commercial profile of Double Power (manufacturer of the DF-8150F) and of BOOS S.A.'s distribution rights, plus the two losing bidders IBANDO / IB&O Intelligent Bank & Office Machines (V. Papadatos & Sia E.E.) and Ch. THEODOSIS A.B.E.E., for Agent 6's competitor file.
- **Romania — SEAP/SICAP (e-licitatie):** TED award notices for SICAP procedures CN1089569 (DGRFP Ploiesti POS framework, RON 9,284,220, awarded) and CN1090221 (DGRFP Brasov, RON 2,170,000, awarded) - needed to name the winning acquiring banks, and not retrievable through the SICAP API because its award-notice filter is broken. Also the full tender dossier for BNR SCN1172961 (HSM support services, RON 335,490, awarded) if an operator has a SICAP login, to identify the incumbent HSM vendor and contract expiry.
- **Bulgaria — CAIS EOP (app.eop.bg):** A browser session on CAIS EOP would close the below-threshold gap TED cannot see. Please sweep these EOP buyer profiles for notices under the EU thresholds, filtering for cash/counting/security/POS: https://app.eop.bg/buyer/11700 (Българска народна банка), https://app.eop.bg/buyer/14878 (Български пощи ЕАД), https://app.eop.bg/buyer/1240 (Столична община). Also worth opening the contract pages https://app.eop.bg/today/533073 (BNB cash-equipment euro adaptation) and https://app.eop.bg/today/439815 (Bulgarian Posts counting machines) for the technical specifications, which name the exact machine models and would let Printec size a service alternative. No paywalled PDFs were encountered.
- **Ukraine — Prozorro (prozorro.gov.ua + tender.privatbank.ua):** None required for the tender data itself — everything cited was fetched directly from the Prozorro CDB. If deeper company data is wanted, the operator route is the Ukrainian company register (YouControl / Opendatabot), which is paywalled, for these EDRPOUs: TOV "ATM sistems" 44372551 (the Ukrgasbank 81-ATM winner, top priority for Agent 6), TOV "SeilServiSolyushens"/SalesServiceSolutions 39906179, TOV "SERVUS SISTEMZ INTEGREYSHN" 31089403, TOV "TK MAYTEK" 37674571, TOV "BANKIVSKI TEKHNOLOHIYI" 33417694, TOV "IT PARK" 38050566, TOV "KARTSYSTEMY" 31747324, TOV "SAYFER PRO" 42125815 and TOV "UKRAINSKI INTELEKTUALNI SYSTEMY" 40016414. Secondarily, the Ukrgasbank qualification-rejection protocol behind the collapsed 08/01/2026 Printec award (UA-2025-11-20-012352-a) sits in the tender's document attachments and would explain why Printec lost this lot on compliance rather than price.
- **Croatia — EOJN (eojn.hr):** 1) HIGH VALUE — obtain the Ernst & Young study commissioned by Hrvatska udruga banaka (HUB) on the three operating models for Croatia's national shared ATM network. Not public; ask HUB (hub.hr) directly or via a Croatian banking contact. This determines whether the 01/01/2027 network is bank-consortium-run, outsourced to an operator, or abandoned in favour of the two-free-withdrawals fallback — i.e. whether Printec's bank-neutral ATM-operator model (Cashflex/KEA in Greece) has a route in. 2) Drive https://eojn.hr/ in a real browser: Postupci -> Jednostavna nabava, search 'bankomat', 'POS', 'brojač novčanica', 'brojač kovanica', 'samoposlužni', 'blagajnič'; capture the XHR endpoint the SPA calls so below-threshold Croatian notices become machine-readable. This is the only way to resolve Hrvatska pošta ref 26.2.83 (POS maintenance) and HNB P-88/P-407. 3) Check DKOM (https://www.dkom.hr/, Javna objava odluka) via a browser for appeals against: HAC procedure 1-300-2026-501 (TED 84363-2026), HNB V-32/2026 CPS 7000i (TED 236172-2026) and FINA 'Brojači kovanica' ref 2.2.1/2026/V/I2 — all three are stalled with no published result. 4) Pull the HNB payment-statistics series for the current number of ATMs and POS terminals in Croatia to firm up the 3,816-ATM / EUR 19.24bn market sizing, which currently rests on a press citation rather than a regulator table. 5) HARNESS ISSUE, not a research request: the orchestrator was told this unit "returned NOTHING", but /Users/mangian/Downloads/BANKING/findings/02-tenders/parts/2026-08-01/croatia-eojn.json exists with status:"complete" and 25 signals. Check the result-collection path before scheduling further retries for this slug.
- **Slovenia — e-narocanje:** none - every document needed was retrievable without credentials or payment. The Banka Slovenije sorting-system tender documentation itself sits behind the e-JN submission system (https://ejn.gov.si/ponudba/pages/aktualno/aktualno_jnc_podrobno.xhtml?zadevaId=76928), which requires a registered supplier account; if Printec wants the technical specification and the OEM-authorisation qualification wording before the 03/08/2026 question deadline, an operator with an e-JN account should download the razpisna dokumentacija for JN005737/2026-EUe16/01.
- **Slovakia — UVO (uvo.gov.sk):** 1) IS EVO login needed: open ÚVO zákazka 558047 'Scanning System' (NBS, created 18/05/2026) at evo.isepvo.sk and report the subject description, CPV code, estimated value and deadline — the public ÚVO page withholds all of them. 2) IS EVO / ERANET login: pull the súťažné podklady for Slovenská pošta 06/VS/2026 (ÚVO 559931, docs at https://www.uvo.gov.sk/vyhladavanie/vyhladavanie-zakaziek/dokumenty/559931, bids via posta.eranet.sk) to see WHICH security systems are in scope before the 14/08/2026 deadline. 3) JOSEPHINE registration: download the NBS LAN tender pack at https://josephine.proebiz.com/sk/tender/79535/summary (deadline 24/08/2026). 4) Ask Slovenská pošta's press office who supplied the five self-service cash kiosks piloted from 15/08/2025 and when the rollout decision is due. 5) Check the Finančné riaditeľstvo SR website for the § 15(3) technical specification of eKasa cashless-payment confirmation — it defines who may supply the confirmation service under Act 384/2025.
- **Czechia — NEN (nen.nipez.cz) + ČNB E-ZAK (ezak.cnb.cz):** 1. TENDER ARENA supplier registration for Ceska posta, s.p. — an operator should register Printec as a supplier at https://tenderarena.cz (support@tendersystems.cz, +420 226 258 888) so that Czech Post's below-threshold cash-equipment tenders, which never reach TED, become visible. This is the single largest blind spot in Czech coverage and it is a registration wall, not a paywall. 2. Statni tiskarna cenin PTK documentation — the biometric-enrolment-kiosk market-consultation pack sits behind https://mfcr.ezak.cz/document_download_44166.html on the Ministry of Finance E-ZAK instance and may require a registered account; an operator should retrieve it, as it contains STC's draft technical and fraud-detection requirements ahead of the CZK 50,000,000 tender. 3. Prague Castle CIT winner — the 'Oznameni o vyberu' selection notice for NEN N006/25/V00036604 is a document attachment on the NEN detail page; an operator should download it to name the incumbent Czech CIT operator.
- **Hungary — EKR (ekr.gov.hu / kozbeszerzes.hu):** 1) Constitutional Court (Alkotmanybirosag) case register — an operator with a real browser should search https://alkotmanybirosag.hu/ugykereso for 'automata bankjegykiado' and '2025. evi XVIII.' to retrieve the official case number(s) for the OTP/Erste/Raiffeisen complaint filed 10/11/2025 and any procedural order or decision; the site is JS-only and returned nothing to fetch/curl. 2) Annex 1 (1. melleklet) of MNB decree 19/2025 (VI. 26.) — the settlement-by-settlement, bank-by-bank package list as published in Magyar Kozlony, to build a targeted Phase-2 (500-1,000 population) site list for direct bank outreach. 3) MNB payment-statistics release for Q2 2026 (ATM count by county) if it is behind a data-portal form.
- **Western Balkans portals (AL/BA/XK/ME/MK):** 1) Register a Printec ECONOMIC OPERATOR account on e-prokurimi.rks-gov.net (Kosovo) — manual at https://e-prokurimi.rks-gov.net/Home/Manuals/Ponuditelji/Regjistrimi%20OE%20-%20manual.pdf — without it the entire Kosovo notice database is invisible. 2) Register a Printec ECONOMIC OPERATOR account on https://bid.app.gov.al (Albania) — same problem. 3) Register an economic-operator account on https://www.e-nabavki.gov.mk (North Macedonia) to receive NBRM notices by email. 4) Request the Bank of Albania tender dossier for "Blerje sistem automatik i paketimit me plastmas te kartemonedhave" (ALL 17,511,600) from avaraku@bankofalbania.org BEFORE 18/08/2026. 5) Email prokurimi@bqk-kos.org for the award results of BQK 057 221 (cash-processing machine maintenance) and BQK 054 121 (cash-processing consumables) — these name the incumbent and reveal the machine make. 6) Email procurement@teb-kos.com for the outcomes of IFB 259/26 (cash-handling machines) and IFB 270/26 (remote digital onboarding + digital signature) — TEB publishes no award notices, so this is the only route to a winner.
- **Bank-owned procurement pages:** 1. PrivatBank PrivatTender, tender PB-2026-03-17-3068329 'Ресайклінговий банкомат (1 етап)': the six qualification documents (Запит кваліфікаційних документів, plus the 30/03/2026 amended version and the English version) are behind a free registration wall. An operator should register a company account on tender.privatbank.ua and download them — they will contain the unit quantity, the technical specification and the pre-qualification criteria for the ~800-unit recycler programme. Same account unlocks the Учасники (participants) and Договори (contracts) tabs, which would name the competitors. 2. TEB Sh.A. Kosovo: award outcomes are never published. An operator should email procurement@teb-kos.com to request the results of 259/26 (cash-handling machines, closed 02/06/2026) and 270/26 (remote digital onboarding, closed 29/06/2026), and ask to be added to the standing bidder list. 3. Banka Poštanska štedionica: email nabavka@posted.co.rs for the outcome of the CIT + ATM-servicing procurement (offers closed 30/04/2026, still 'У току' after three months). 4. CEC Bank Romania: CEC accepts offers only on paper at Calea Victoriei 13, Bucharest; an operator should register Printec with sia@cec.ro (021 311 11 19) as a candidate supplier for ATM/self-service, cash automation and managed services. 5. No paywalled PDFs blocked this run.
- **Austria — ausschreibungen.usp.gv.at (OeNB & public buyers):** 1) ANKOe / vergabeportal.at registration: an operator with a registered Printec supplier account should download the tender documents for GSA 'Kombinierte Ein- und Auszahlungsgeraete', https://gv.vergabeportal.at/Detail/244277 (internal ref G26146096) — technical specification, lot structure, device volumes and whether the candidate shortlist is disclosed. This is the EUR 9.1m recycler opportunity and is the single highest-value document in this unit. 2) Same portal, https://gv.vergabeportal.at/Detail/252147 — Muenze Oesterreich coin-blank weighing line, closing 01/09/2026. 3) Confirm internally whether Printec (or any Printec entity) submitted a Teilnahmeantrag for TED 305531-2026 by 08/06/2026.
- **Cyprus — eprocurement.gov.cy:** 1) KEDIPES (Cyprus Asset Management Company): retrieve the full documents for TENDER NO. 14/2026 "Purchase, Installation, Maintenance and Support of NAC & Switches" (offer deadline extended to 22/07/2026 12:00) and TENDER PROC. NO. 13/2026 "Impairment and Fair Value Calculations under IFRS 9". Both detail pages under https://kedipes.com.cy/en/tenders/ now return 404 and the listing is JS-rendered with an empty payload — ask KEDIPES directly or capture from a live authenticated browser session. 2) Central Bank of Cyprus: request the outcome of Tender 02/2026 (SIEM, EUR 370,000 excl. VAT, bids closed 24/04/2026) from cbctenders@centralbank.cy or the Official Gazette — awarded, cancelled or relaunched, and to whom. No public channel discloses it. 3) Deputy Ministry of Research, Innovation & Digital Policy: request the contract value and term of ΥΕΚΨΠ/2024/13/Δ/Δ (electronic identities for citizens over 18, awarded to JCC Payment Systems Ltd on 04/04/2025); both bid cycles were lodged off-system so no price appears on e-PPS.
